on December 18, 2016, at the [[Sibur Arena]] in [[Saint Petersburg]], [[Russia]].\n\n===Background===\nA Lightweight bout between Musa Khamanaev and Saul Rogers was scheduled for this card. However, the fight was cancelled in early December due to an injury sustained by Rogers, the bout will be Rescheduled.\n\n[[Vyacheslav Vasilevsky]] Not Medically Cleared to fight, his bout with Albert Duraev was canceled.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n\nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=https://acbmma.com/en/news/bonus-10-000-asv-50-stormbringer-5566_237|title=Bonus $ 10 [[Russia]]\n|}\n\n==ACB 51: Silva vs. Torgeson==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 51: Silva vs. Torgeson \n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=January 13, 2017\n|venue=[[Bren Events Center]] \n|city={{flagicon|USA}} [[Irvine, California]], [[United States|USA]]\n|attendance= ~ 500\n|gate= \n|previousevent=[[2016 in Absolute Championship Berkut#ACB 50: Stormbringer|ACB 50]]\n|followingevent=[[#ACB 52: Another Level of MMA Fighting|ACB 52]] \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 51: Silva vs. Torgeson''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on January 13, 2017, at the [[Bren Events Center]] in [[Irvine, California]] [[United States]].\n\n===Background===\nThis event featured a world title fight for the vacant [[Absolute Championship Berkut#Light Heavyweight Championship|ACB Light Heavyweight Championship]] between [[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] and Jared Ferguson as headliner.\n\nA Heavyweight bout between [[Micha\u0142 Andryszak]] and [[Mike Kyle]] was scheduled for this card. However, the fight was cancelled due to visa problem sustained by Andryszak.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n \nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:{{cite 52: Another Level of MMA Fighting==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 52: Another Level of MMA Fighting \n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=January 21, 2017\n|venue=Hallmann dome\n|city={{flagicon|AUT}} [[Vienna]], [[Austria]]\n|attendance= ~ 2,000\n|gate= \n|previousevent=[[#ACB 51: Silva vs. Torgeson|ACB 51]]\n|followingevent=[[#ACB 53: Young Eagles 15|ACB 53]] \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 52: Another Level of MMA Fighting''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on January 21, 2017, at the Hallmann dome in [[Vienna]], [[Austria]].\n\n===Background===\n6 fighters from Russia, who were to perform at the ACB 52 tournament in Vienna, have not been able to participate due to visa problems.\n\nThe fight between Patrik Kincl and Arbi Agujev was originally a 2nd-round TKO win by Kincl. The fight was overturned in a No Contest by the ACB official, because the referee stopped the fight too soon.\n\nThe fight between Jo\u00e3o Luis Nogueira and Shamil Nikaev was originally a unanimous decision win by Nogueira. The judges decision was Overturned in a No Contest by the ACB official.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n \nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n\n*'''Knockout of the Night: Ismail Naurdiev'''\n*'''Submission of the Night: Matjaz Vicar'''\n\n===Result===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; \n|\n|}\n\n==ACB 54: Supersonic==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 54: Supersonic\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=March 11, 2017\n|venue=[[Manchester Arena]]\n|city={{flagicon|ENG}} [[Manchester]], [[England]]\n|attendance=~10,000\n|gate= \n|previousevent=[[#ACB 53: Young Eagles 15|ACB 53]]\n|followingevent=[[#Absolute Championship Berkut 55|ACB 55]] \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 54: Supersonic''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on March 11, 2017, at the [[Manchester Arena]] in [[Manchester]], [[England]].\n\n===Background===\nThis event featured a Champion vs. Champion fight between The [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Middleweight championship|KSW Middleweight Champion]] [[Mamed Khalidov]] and the Venator FC Middleweight Champion [[Luke Barnatt]] as headliner, Also the event was originally scheduled to be co-headlined by a welterweight title fight between [[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]] and Aslambek Saidov. However, on February 14, it was announced that Cooper had to withdraw from his first title defense due to serious health issues. Saidov has faced ACB newcomer Ion Pascu. Lightweight Pat Healy and light heavyweight Vinny Magalhaes both suffered injuries in the lead up to the event, and they will no longer participate in the event.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n\nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=https://acbmma.com/en/news/acb-54-bonus-10-000-aiqwCDF3_360|title=Bonus 10000$ ACB 54|last=acbmma.com|access-date=2017-03-14|archive-date=2017-03-14|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170314154802/https://acbmma.com/en/news/acb-54-bonus-10-000-aiqwCDF3_360|url-status=dead}}\n\n*'''Fight Barnatt|publisher=flocombat.com|date=2016-11-25|access-date=2016-11-25}}\n\n==ACB 55: Dushanbe==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 55: Dushanbe\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=March 24, 2017\n|venue= Tax Committee Sports Center \n|city={{flagicon|TJK}} [[Dushanbe]], [[Tajikistan]]\n|attendance= \n|gate= \n|previousevent=[[#ACB 54: Supersonic|ACB Sports Center in [[Dushanbe]], [[Tajikistan]].\n\n===Background===\nGamzat Khiramagomedov was injured during the preparation for his fight and was forced to withdraw from his match against Will Noland.{{Cite web|url=http://mmaoctagon.ru/news/boy-khiramagomedov-noland-na-acb-55-otmenen/ |title=Khiramagomedov vs Noland canceled |publisher=mmaoctagon.ru |url-status=dead |archive-date=March 24, 2017 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170324180032/http://mmaoctagon.ru/news/boy-khiramagomedov-noland-na-acb-55-otmenen/ }}\n\nEvgeniy Lazukov was injured and couldn't participate in his Fight against Muin Gafurov, and was subsequently replaced by Georgian fighter Beno Adamia.\n\n[[Donald Sanchez]] and Abdul-Rahman Temirov has to withdraw due to injury, Yusuf Raisov and valdines Silva will now face each other in the co-main event of the evening.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n \nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night: Shakhbulatov vs. Macek'''\n*'''Submission of the Night: Igor Fernandes'''\n*'''ACB League Founder Bonus : Mukhamed Berkhamov'''\n\n===Result===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" (Punches)\n|align=center|3\n|align=center|4:48\n|\n|}\n\n==ACB 57: Payback==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 57: Payback\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=April 15, 2017\n|venue= [[Luzhniki Palace of Sports]] \n|city={{flagicon|RUS}} [[Moscow]], [[Russia]]\n|attendance= ~ 10,000\n|gate= \n|previousevent=[[#ACB 56: Young Eagles 16|ACB 56]] \n|followingevent=[[#ACB 58: Young Eagles 17|ACB 58]] \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 57: Payback''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on April 15, 2017, at the [[Luzhniki Palace of Sports]] in [[Moscow]], [[Russia]].\n\n===Background===\nThis event featured the highly anticipated rematch for the [[Absolute Championship Berkut#Bantamweight Championship|ACB Bantamweight Championship]] between [[Magomed Magomedov (fighter)|Magomed Magomedov]] and [[Petr Yan]] as headliner.\n \nThe title fight between [[Abdul-Aziz Abdulvakhabov]] and. Andrey Koshkin is off. The Bout will be Rescheduled to [[#ACB 61|ACB 61]] due to Abdulvakhabov knee injury.\n\nMatheus Mattos will replace Tural Ragimov in fight against Magomed Ginazov.\n\nAlbert Duraev is out due to Illness, Ibragim Chuzhigaev will step in as a short notice replacement against [[Vyacheslav Vasilevsky]].\n\nKonstantin Erokhin is out due to injury, Georgy Sakaev will step in as a short notice replacement against [[Azamat Murzakanov]].\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n \nThe following fighters were awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night: Ginazov vs. Mattos'''\n*'''Submission of the Night: web|url=https://acbmma.com/en/tournamentnext/acb-62-rostov-don-aehmvxyR_74|title=ACB 62 - Rostov-on-Don|last=medialead.ru}}{{Dead link|date=September 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot Shabily. Unfortunately, Townsend came in on the scales at 162 lbs. The bout against Shabily was canceled.\n\n'''Bonus awards:'''\n \nThe following fighters will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night: Valeriy Khazhirokov vs Isaac Agujev vs. Alfaya==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 79: Agujev vs. Alfaya\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=January 27, 2018\n|venue=Sports Hall Coliseum\n|city={{flagicon|RUS}} [[Grozny]], [[Russia]]\n|attendance=\n|gate=\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 79: Agujev vs. Alfaya''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on January 27, 2018 at the Sports Hall Coliseum in [[Grozny]], [[Russia]].\n\n'''Background'''\nThe card was originally headlined by a title fight between champion Askar Askarov and Rasul Albaskhanov for the ACB Flyweight Championship. On January 13, it was announced Askarov had to withdraw due to heavy angina. The title fight between Askarov and Albaskhanov has been temporarily postponed.\n\nBatraz Agnaev had to withdraw due to an injury and is not able to defend his Light-Heavyweight title against D\u00f6wletjan \u00ddag\u015fymyradow from [[Turkmenistan]] and the bout was canceled. Luis Fernando Miranda will step in as a replacement against \u00ddag\u015fymyradow.\n\nMagomed Magomedov was to face Walter Pereira Jr. at this event but had to withdraw due to illness. The bout has been temporarily postponed to ACB 81.\n\nIslam Isaev, Said-Khamzat Avkhadov, Thiago Bonifacio Silva and [[Zach Makovsky]] were injured during training camp. So they were removed from the card.\n\nBonus awards:\n \nThe following fighter will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Submission of the Night: Rasul Shovkhalov'''\n*'''$5000 Stoppage Victory Bonuses: Husein Kushagov, Magomed Ginazov, [[Dovletdzhan Yagshimuradov]] and Arbi Agujev'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; 80: Tumenov vs. Burrell==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACB 80: Tumenov vs. Burrell\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]] \n|date=February 16, 2018\n|venue=[[Basket-Hall Burrell''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on February 16, 2018 at the [[Basket-Hall Krasnodar|Basket-Hall]] in [[Krasnodar]], [[Russia]].\n\n'''Background'''\nThe card was originally headlined by a title fight between champion [[Mukhamed Berkhamov]] and [[Albert Tumenov]] for the [[Absolute Championship Berkut#Welterweight Championship|ACB Welterweight Championship]]. On January 1, it was announced Berkhamov had to withdraw due to a broken arm. [[Nah-Shon Burrell]] will step in as a replacement against Tumenov.\n\nInitially Muhammed Kokov was supposed to face [[Bubba Jenkins]], but Jenkins withdrew from the fight due to injury. The Brazilian [[Alexandre Bezerra]] will step in as a replacement against Kokov.\n\nBonus awards:\n \nThe following fighters will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night: Daniel Santos vs. Burrell\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Berkut]]\n|date=April 7, 2018\n|venue=[[Ondrej Nepela Arena]]\n|city={{flagicon|SVK}} [[Bratislava]], [[Slovakia]]\n|attendance=\n|gate= \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 84: Burrell vs. Agujev''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on April 7, 2018 at the [[Ondrej Nepela Arena]] in [[Bratislava]], [[Slovakia]].\n\n'''Background'''\nThe card was originally headlined by a fight between [[Attila V\u00e9gh]] and Jose Daniel Toledo. On March 30, it was announced Vegh had to withdraw because he torn his meniscus. [[Ron Stallings]] will step in as a replacement against Toledo.\n\nLudovit Klein had to withdraw due to injury, [[Luis Nogueira|Luis Alberto Nogueira]] will step in to face Islam Sizbulatov.\n\nBonus awards:\n \nThe following fighters will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night: Joshua Aveles vs. Adrian Zieli\u0144ski'''\n*'''Knockout of the Night: Yusup Umarov'''\n*'''Submission of \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Berkut 86: Balaev vs. Raisov 2''''' will be a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Berkut on May 5, 2018 at the [[Olympic Stadium (Moscow)|Olimpiyskiy]] in [[Moscow]], [[Russia]] .\n\n'''Background'''\nThe title fight between [[Abdul-Aziz Abdulvakhabov]] and Ustarmagomed Gadzhidaudov got cancelled due to Gadzhidaudov injury.\n\n[[Mukhamed Berkhamov]] had to withdraw due to an injury and is not able to defend his Welterweight title against Beslan Isaev and the bout was canceled. Ciro Rodrigues step up on to face Isaev.\n\n[[Eduard Vartanyan]] had to pull out of his fight against [[Ali Bagov]] due to a tore ligaments in his knee. Gleristone Santos step up on short notice to face Bagov.\n\nThe fight between Abdul-Rakhman Temirov and Rasuk Mirzaev got cancelled due to Mirzaev injury. Denis Silva step up on short notice to face Temirov.\n\nThe fight between Akop Stepanyan and Arman Ospanov got cancelled due to Stepanyan illnesses.\n\nBonus awards:\n \nThe following fighters will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' Koshkin vs Shovkhalov\n*'''Knockout of the Night:''' Abdul-Rakhman Temirov \n*'''Submission of the Night:''' Yusuf Raisov\n*'''$5000 Stoppage compete in Australia, [[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] could not make the trip. His fight against [[Chris Camozzi]] was canceled.\n\n[[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]] had to withdraw of his fight against Adam Townsend due to an injury. Kieran Joblin step up on short notice to face Townsend.\n\n[[Luis Palomino]] had to pull out of his fight against Andrew Fisher due to visa issue. Rodolfo Marquez step up on short notice to face Fisher. Fisher has also been forced to withdraw from the card on medical grounds.\n\nRaymison Bruno is out, Andy Young steps in on six days notice to take on Narek Avagyan.\n\nBonus awards:\n \nThe following fighters will be awarded $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' Luke Barnatt vs. Karol Celinski\n*'''Submission of the Night:''' Mindaugas Verzbickas\n*'''$5000 Stoppage Victory Bonuses:''' Marcin Held, Rodolfo Vieira, Kyle Reyes, Narek Avagyan\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n! \nDue to the cancellation of multiple events, numbering of the future events has been changed. withdrew for health reasons.''\n\n==ACA 169: Bibulatov vs. De\u00e1k==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACA 169: Bibulatov vs. De\u00e1k\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Akhmat]] \n|date=January 26, 2024\n|venue=Sports Hall Coliseum \n|city={{flagicon|RUS}} [[Grozny]], Russia \n|attendance= \n|gate= \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent= \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Akhmat 169: Bibulatov vs. De\u00e1k''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Akhmat on January 26, 2024 at the Sports Hall Coliseum in [[Grozny]], Russia.\n\n===Background===\n\nAn [[Absolute Championship Akhmat#Bantamweight Championship|ACA Bantamweight Championship]] rematch between reigning champion Oleg Borisov and former champion [[Magomed Bibulatov]] was expected to headline the event.{{cite web |title=\u0427\u0435\u043c\u043f\u0438\u043e\u043d \u0410\u0421\u0410 \u0411\u043e\u0440\u0438\u0441\u043e\u0432 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0434\u0435\u0442 \u0440\u0435\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0448 \u0441 \u0411\u0438\u0431\u0443\u043b\u0430\u0442\u043e\u0432\u044b\u043c 26 \u044f\u043d\u0432\u0430\u0440\u044f \u0432 \u0413\u0440\u043e\u0437\u043d\u043e\u043c |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/chempion-asa-borisov-provedet-revansh-s-bibulatovym-26-yanvarya-v-groznom-311411/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=December 2, 2023}} The pairing previously met at [[2022 in Absolute Championship Akhmat#ACA 138: Vagaev vs Gadzhidaudov|ACA 138]] on March 26, 2022, where Borisov capture the title via unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://fighttime.ru/events/96498/ACA-138-Absolute-Championship-Akhmat-Vagaev-vs-Gadzhidaudov.html|title=ACA 138: \u0412\u0430\u0433\u0430\u0435\u0432 - \u0413\u0430\u0434\u0436\u0438\u0434\u0430\u0443\u0434\u043e\u0432|website=fighttime.ru|access-date=2022-03-27}} However, champion Borisov withdrew from both the fight and the grand prix due to injury and was replaced by Tom\u00e1\u0161 De\u00e1k in a non-title semi-final bout..{{cite web |title=\u0422\u043e\u043c\u0430\u0448 \u0414\u044d\u043a \u0437\u0430\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0442 \u041e\u043b\u0435\u0433\u0430 \u0411\u043e\u0440\u0438\u0441\u043e\u0432\u0430 \u0432 \u043f\u043e\u043b\u0443\u0444\u0438\u043d\u0430\u043b\u0435 \u0413\u0440\u0430\u043d-\u041f\u0440\u0438 ACA \u0432 \u043b\u0435\u0433\u0447\u0430\u0439\u0448\u0435\u043c \u0432\u0435\u0441\u0435 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/tomash-dek-zamenit-olega-borisova-v-polufinale-gran-pri-aca-v-legchaishem-vese-324591/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=January 16, 2024}} \n\nA semi-final bout for the [[2023_in_Absolute_Championship_Akhmat#2023_Grands_Prix|2023 ACA Flyweight Grand Prix]] between [[Azamat Kerefov]] and Rasul Albaskhanov was expected to serve as the co-main event. However Kerefov withdrew from the bout for health reasons and was replaced by Azamat Pshukov.{{cite web |title=\u041a\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0444\u043e\u0432 \u0432\u044b\u0431\u044b\u043b \u0441 \u0431\u043e\u044f \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0442\u0438\u0432 \u0410\u043b\u0431\u0430\u0441\u0445\u0430\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0430 \u043d\u0430 ACA 169, \u0435\u0433\u043e \u0437\u0430\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0442 \u041f\u0448\u0443\u043a\u043e\u0432 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/kerefov-vybyl-s-boya-protiv-albaskhanova-na-aca-169-ego-zamenit-pushkov-321283/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=December 2, 2023}} \n\n'''Bonus awards:''' \n\nThe following fighters were awarded bonuses:\n*'''$50,000 Performance of the Night:''' Murad Abdulaev \n*'''$25,000 Performance of the Night:''' Khusein Kushagov\n*'''$5000 Stoppage Victory Bonuses:''' [[Magomed Bibulatov]], Azamat Pshukov, Amirkhan Adaev, Albert Gukov, Dzhambulat Zelimkhanov, Akhmed 170: Goncharov vs. Vyazigin 2==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACA 170: Goncharov vs. Vyazigin 2\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Akhmat]] \n|date=February 9, 2024\n|venue=[[Irina Vyazigin 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Akhmat on February 9, 2024 at the [[Irina Viner-Usmanova Gymnastics Palace]] in [[Moscou]], Russia.\n\n===Background===\n\nAn [[Absolute Championship Akhmat#Heavyweight Championship|ACA Heavyweight Championship]] bout between reigning champion [[Evgeniy Goncharov]] and challenger Anton Vyazigin headlined the event.{{cite web |title=\u0422\u0438\u0442\u0443\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u0431\u043e\u0439 \u0413\u043e\u043d\u0447\u0430\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0430 \u0438 \u0412\u044f\u0437\u0438\u0433\u0438\u043d\u0430 \u0441\u043e\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0438\u0442\u0441\u044f 9 \u0444\u0435\u0432\u0440\u0430\u043b\u044f \u043d\u0430 \u0410\u0421\u0410 170 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/titulnyi-boi-goncharova-i-vyazigina-sostoitsya-9-fevralya-na-asa-170-320066/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=December 29, 2023}} The pairing previously met at RCC 8 on December 19, 2020, where Goncharov won via unanimous decision.\n\nAn [[Absolute Championship Akhmat#Flyweight Championship|ACA Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion Kurban Gadzhiev and contender Azam Gaforov was expected to take place in the co-main event.{{cite web |title=\u0422\u0438\u0442\u0443\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u0431\u043e\u0439 \u0432 \u0444\u0435\u0432\u0440\u0430\u043b\u0435 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/titulnyi-boi-gaforova-i-gadzhieva-proidet-v-ramkakh-aca-170-v-fevrale-320268/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=December 30, 2023}} However, champion Gadzhiev withdrew from both the fight and the grand prix due to injury.{{cite web |title=\u0413\u0430\u0444\u043e\u0440\u043e\u0432 \n}}\n\n'''''Absolute Championship Akhmat 172: Esengulov vs. Vagaev''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by Absolute Championship Akhmat on March 9, 2024 at the [[Irina Viner-Usmanova Gymnastics Palace]] in [[Moscow]], Russia.\n\n===Background===\n\nA Middleweight rematch between Magomed Ismailov and former [[Bellator Middleweight World Championship|Bellator Middleweight]] title challenger [[Anatoly Tokov]] was expected to headline the event.{{cite web |title=\u041e\u043f\u0443\u0431\u043b\u0438\u043a\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d \u043e\u0444\u0438\u0446\u0438\u0430\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u043f\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0435\u0440 \u0440\u0435\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0448\u0430 \u0418\u0441\u043c\u0430\u0438\u043b\u043e\u0432\u0430 \u0438 \u0422\u043e\u043a\u043e\u0432\u0430 \u043d\u0430 ACA 172 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/opublikovan-oficialnyi-poster-revansha-ismailova-i-tokova-na-aca-172-326481/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=January 22, 2024}}The pairing previously met at M-1 Challenge 39 on May 23, 2013, where Ismailov won via submission in the first round. However, Magomedov withdrew from the fight due to knee injury.{{cite web |title=\u0418\u0441\u043c\u0430\u0438\u043b\u043e\u0432 \u043f\u043e\u043b\u0443\u0447\u0438\u043b \u0442\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043c\u0443, \u0431\u043e\u0439 \u0441 \u0422\u043e\u043a\u043e\u0432\u044b\u043c \u043e\u0442\u043b\u043e\u0436\u0435\u043d \u043d\u0430 \u043d\u0435\u043e\u043f\u0440\u0435\u0434\u0435\u043b\u0435\u043d\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u0441\u0440\u043e\u043a |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/ismailov-poluchil-travmu-boi-s-tokovym-otlozhen-na-neopredelennyi-srok-340384/ |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru |date=March 4, 2024}} As a results, a semi-final bout for the [[2024 in Absolute Championship Akhmat#2023 ACA Welterweight Grand Prix|2023 ACA Welterweight Grand Prix]] between Edil Esengulov and Abubakar Vagaev was promoted to the main event status.\n\n'''Bonus awards:''' \n\nThe following fighters were awarded bonuses:\n*'''$50,000 Performance of the Night:''' Abubakar Vagaev and Adlan Ibragimov\n*'''$25,000 Performance of the Night:''' Leonardo Silva\n*'''$5000 Stoppage Victory Bonuses:''' Adam Bogatyrev and Aren Akopyan\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" |website=ACA MMA |language=ru}}\n\n==ACA 199: Frolov vs. Emeev==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ACA 199: Frolov vs. Emeev\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Absolute Championship Akhmat]] \n|date=January 16, 2026\n|venue=[[Basket-Hall Krasnodar]] \n|city=[[Krasnodar]], Russia \n|attendance= \n|gate= \n|previousevent=\n|followingevent= \n}}\n'''''ACA 199: Frolov vs. Emeev''''' is an upcoming [[mixed martial arts]] event promoted by [[Absolute Championship Akhmat]] that will take place on January 16, 2026, in [[Krasnodar]], Russia.\n\n===Background===\nA lightweight bout between Alexey Polpudnikov and Rasul Magomedov was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web |last=\u0418\u0441\u043a\u0435\u043d\u0434\u0435\u0440\u043e\u0432 |first=\u0411\u0430\u0445\u0442\u0438\u044f\u0440 |date=2025-12-02 |title=\u041f\u043e\u043b\u043f\u0443\u0434\u043d\u0438\u043a\u043e\u0432 \u0438 \u041c\u0430\u0433\u043e\u043c\u0435\u0434\u043e\u0432 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0434\u0443\u0442 \u0431\u043e\u0439 16 \u044f\u043d\u0432\u0430\u0440\u044f \u043d\u0430 \u0442\u0443\u0440\u043d\u0438\u0440\u0435 ACA 199 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/polpudnikov-i-magomedov-provedut-boi-16-yanvarya-na-turnire-aca-199-555960/ |access-date=2025-12-22 |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru}} At the weigh-ins, Polpudnikov weighed in at 73,75 kg, 2,45 kg over the limit. As a results, the bout has been officially cancelled by ACA president [[Magomed Bibulatov]], citing Polpudnikov's serious violation of regulations during the weigh-in.{{Cite web |date=2026-01-16 |title=\u041f\u043e\u043b\u043f\u0443\u0434\u043d\u0438\u043a\u043e\u0432 \u0441\u043e\u0440\u0432\u0430\u043b \u0432\u0437\u0432\u0435\u0448\u0438\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0434 ACA 199: \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0432\u0435\u0441 \u043f\u043e\u0447\u0442\u0438 2,5 \u043a\u0433 |url=https://sportify.press/fighting/aca-199-sryv-vzveshivaniya-pereves/ |access-date=2026-01-16 |website=Sportify.Press |language=ru}}{{Cite web |date=2026-01-16 |title=\u041c\u0430\u0433\u043e\u043c\u0435\u0434 \u0411\u0438\u0431\u0443\u043b\u0430\u0442\u043e\u0432 \u0441\u043e\u043e\u0431\u0449\u0438\u043b \u043e \u0441\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0435 \u0433\u043b\u0430\u0432\u043d\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0431\u043e\u044f \u043d\u0430 ACA 199 |url=https://sportify.press/fighting/aca-199-smena-glavnogo-boya-frolov-emeev |access-date=2026-01-16 |website=Sportify.Press |language=ru}}\n\nA middleweight bout between Artem Frolov and UFC veteran [[Ramazan Emeev]] was originally scheduled to headline the event, but the bout was shifted to the co-main event instead.{{Cite web |last=\u0418\u0441\u043a\u0435\u043d\u0434\u0435\u0440\u043e\u0432 |first=\u0411\u0430\u0445\u0442\u0438\u044f\u0440 |date=2025-12-02 |title=\u0424\u0440\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0432 \u0438 \u042d\u043c\u0435\u0435\u0432 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0434\u0443\u0442 \u0431\u043e\u0439 16 \u044f\u043d\u0432\u0430\u0440\u044f \u043d\u0430 \u0442\u0443\u0440\u043d\u0438\u0440\u0435 ACA 199 |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/frolov-i-emeev-provedut-boi-16-yanvarya-na-turnire-aca-199-555706/ |access-date=2025-12-02 |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru}} After the main event was cancelled, the bout was promoted to new headliner.\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $20,000 bonus.
